# Graph Report - investment-agent-main  (2026-09-22)

## Corpus Check
- 1385 files · ~1,686,066 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 18717 nodes · 42199 edges · 1141 communities (650 shown, 451 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 96% EXTRACTED · 4% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 1481 edges (avg confidence: 0.91)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `494de6b2`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
